Here is the full research report on Unturned structure noclip and vehicle flying exploits, organized by topic.

---

## 1. STRUCTURE/BARRICADE COLLISION: SERVER-SIDE vs CLIENT-SIDE

**Architecture**: Unturned uses Unity's physics layer system with 32 layers. The critical layers are:

| Layer | Name | Purpose |
|-------|------|---------|
| 9 | Player | Character capsule -- **all players server-side, local player only client-side** |
| 21 | Clip | Invisible collision for Player and Vehicle |
| 26 | Vehicle | All vehicle colliders |
| 27 | Barricade | Barricades placed in world |
| 28 | Structure | Structures placed in world |

**Key finding from official modding docs**: "Collisions are primarily useful for server-side objects as collisions are not triggered by other players client-side, but this limitation may be resolved in the future." This means object collision (barricades, structures) is server-side, but player-to-player collision is NOT triggered client-side.

**The `Has_Clip_Prefab` property** (bool, defaults `true`) controls whether a barricade uses a **separate server and client prefab**. When `false`, the server and client use the same prefab. This means the server CAN have a different collision mesh than what the client sees.

**`Allow_Collision_While_Animating`** (bool, defaults `false`): When enabled, animated colliders (doors) remain active during animation even when a player overlaps them. The official docs explicitly warn this "can allow for physics-based exploits such as those involving doors."

**Confirmed door-based exploits** were filed as GitHub issues:
- Issue #43: "Raiding Door Glitch" -- placing two plates in a specific way to glitch through doors/walls into bases
- Issue #555: "Glitch into bases using door" -- placing a doorframe next to an unclaimed base and opening the door to clip inside

Both issues were closed, suggesting patches, but the underlying collision architecture remains the same.

**Sources**:
- [Layers -- Unturned 3.x documentation](https://docs.smartlydressedgames.com/en/stable/assets/layers.html)
- [Barricade Assets -- Unturned 3.x documentation](https://docs.smartlydressedgames.com/en/stable/items/barricade-asset.html)
- [Mod Hooks -- Unturned 3 Knowledgebase](https://unturned.info/Modding/ModHooks/)
- [Issue #43 -- Raiding Door Glitch](https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/43)
- [Issue #555 -- Glitch into bases using door](https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/555)
- [Issue #3666 -- Invisible barricades](https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/3666)

---

## 2. PLAYER MOVEMENT: CLIENT-AUTHORITATIVE WITH SERVER CHECKS

**Player movement uses Unity's `CharacterController`**, which runs locally on the client. The server maintains a capsule for every player (layer 9 exists for "all players server-side") but movement is fundamentally **client-authoritative**.

**`Can_Trust_Client_Movement`** is the critical server config setting (defaults `false`). When false, the server validates client-reported positions against server-side expectations. The official documentation states: "Most cheats don't have noclipping, flyhacking, and speedhacking features since this setting is false by default." When servers set this to `true` (to reduce rubberbanding), noclip/fly/speed exploits work freely.

**Server-side movement validation** involves:
- Position difference between ticks vs. expected speed
- Input packet timing (fake lag detection, threshold 3 seconds by default)
- Input spam detection (configurable kick)
- When position mismatches occur, the client is teleported back to the server position. After a recent update, the client can locally re-simulate unacknowledged inputs to eliminate rubberbanding.

**Sources**:
- [Launch Options -- Unturned 3.x documentation](https://docs.smartlydressedgames.com/en/stable/about/launch-options.html)
- [Gameplay config -- Official Unturned Wiki](https://unturned.wiki.gg/wiki/Gameplay_config)
- [Config.json Explained -- Restore Monarchy](https://restoremonarchy.com/blog/ush05-config-json-explained-server-section)

---

## 3. VEHICLE MOVEMENT: CLIENT-AUTHORITATIVE (NOT SERVER-AUTHORITATIVE)

This is the most important finding. The official SDG documentation for the `-ForceTrustClient` launch option states:

&gt; "Disables movement validation (e.g., position difference between ticks matches speed) for vehicles. Not recommended. It is easier for cheaters to fly cars with movement limits disabled. This flag should eventually be removed when(/if) vehicle movement is made server authoritative."

The "when(/if)" phrasing confirms: **vehicle movement is currently client-authoritative, and the transition to server-authoritative is uncertain/low priority.**

**Server-side speed sanity checks** (introduced in patch 3.20.5.0, May 2020):

| Property | Purpose | Default |
|----------|---------|---------|
| `Valid_Speed_Horizontal` | XZ plane speed cap | `(Speed_Max * 0.125)^2` for helicopters, `(Speed_Max * 0.1)^2` for others |
| `Valid_Speed_Up` | Upward vertical speed cap | (per vehicle) |
| `Valid_Speed_Down` | Downward vertical speed cap | 25 m/s for cars/boats, 100 m/s for others |

When exceeded, the server rejects the movement state. Community issue #4236 documents the observable symptom: vehicles "freeze in place after accelerating to speed higher than 79 kph" and "teleport back and forth for a second or two" -- this only happens in multiplayer, confirming server-side enforcement.

**Vehicle client-server desync** (Issue #2906): Helicopters experience positional misalignment where the server-side collision clip drifts from the client's vehicle position, particularly at high speeds with angled movement. The server attempts corrections every few seconds. This is a fundamental synchronization problem within the client-authoritative vehicle physics model.

**Sources**:
- [Launch Options -- Unturned 3.x documentation](https://docs.smartlydressedgames.com/en/stable/about/launch-options.html)
- [Vehicle Assets -- Unturned 3.x documentation](https://docs.smartlydressedgames.com/en/latest/assets/vehicle-asset.html)
- [Issue #4236 -- Vehicle stops on server](https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/4236)
- [Issue #2906 -- Vehicle client-server desync](https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/2906)
- [Vehicle Physics Profile -- Unturned 3 Knowledgebase](https://unturned.info/Modding/VehiclePhysicsProfile/)

---

## 4. KNOWN NOCLIP TECHNIQUES

**Universal Unity CharacterController noclip**: Set `CharacterController.radius = float.PositiveInfinity`. Unity cannot calculate collisions with infinite radius, so the player passes through all geometry. For Unturned (Mono-based), the field chain is:
`Player -&gt; mySkinName -&gt; firstPersonControl -&gt; character (CharacterController)`

Injection via dnSpy: `this.mySkinName.firstPersonControl.character.radius = float.PositiveInfinity;`

**EgguWare flight implementation** (from open source): The `PlayerFlight()` method:
- Sets `itemGravityMultiplier = 0` to eliminate gravity
- Directly manipulates player position via transform, bypassing the CharacterController entirely
- Speed controlled by configurable multiplier
- Also modifies `PlayerMovement.SPEED_STAND`, `SPEED_SPRINT`, `SPEED_PRONE`, and `JUMP` fields via reflection (defaults: 4.5, 7.0, 1.5, 7.0)

**Sources**:
- [Universal-Unity-NoClip](https://github.com/jbro129/Universal-Unity-NoClip)
- [EgguWare-Unturned source](https://github.com/Coopyy/EgguWare-Unturned)
- [EgguWare Unrestricted.cs](https://github.com/Coopyy/EgguWare-Unturned/blob/master/Cheat/Cheats/Unrestricted.cs)

---

## 5. KNOWN VEHICLE FLY TECHNIQUES

**Rigidbody gravity toggle**: Set `useGravity = false` on the vehicle's Rigidbody component. Documented in Issue #2697: "Cheaters fly with cars. They do this by use `useGravity = false` inside the vehicle rigidbody." The issue was closed but no built-in fix was documented; admins were directed to third-party plugins.

**Vehicle noclip through structures**: Issue #3816 documents cars passing through all walls/structures when the vehicle exceeds the player's render distance. When local rendering unloads structure colliders but the vehicle physics continues, collision ceases. This is a client-side physics issue.

**Sources**:
- [Issue #2697 -- Anti Cheat for servers](https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/2697)
- [Issue #3816 -- Car going through walls](https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/3816)

---

## 6. ANTI-CHEAT ARCHITECTURE

### BattlEye (Layer 1 -- Kernel-Level, Client-Side)

BEDaisy.sys kernel driver with these detection mechanisms:

| Mechanism | Implementation |
|-----------|---------------|
| Process monitoring | `PsSetCreateProcessNotifyRoutineEx` -- identifies game as first child of BEService.exe |
| Thread injection detection | `PsSetCreateThreadNotifyRoutine` -- flags thread start addresses outside loaded module ranges |
| Module load monitoring | `PsSetLoadImageNotifyRoutine` |
| Handle stripping | `ObRegisterCallbacks` -- strips `PROCESS_VM_READ`/`PROCESS_VM_WRITE` from handles to game process |
| Memory scanning | `KeStackAttachProcess` + MDL operations for cross-process memory reads |
| APC injection | `KeInitializeApc`/`KeInsertQueueApc` into target threads, then `RtlWalkFrameChain` stack walking |
| Code obfuscation | 7.4 MB `.be0` section uses VMProtect-style code virtualization |
| Anti-debug | Checks `KdDebuggerEnabled` at init, refuses to start under kernel debugger |
| Filesystem monitoring | Minifilter at altitude 321000 |

BattlEye signature scanning updates are server-pushed (no game patch required). First deployment on Unturned banned approximately 3,700 confirmed cheaters in the first week.

### VAC (Layer 2)

Standard Valve Anti-Cheat, secondary to BattlEye. Both default to enabled.

### Server-Side Validation (Layer 3)

The built-in movement validation (`Can_Trust_Client_Movement = false` by default) is the primary barrier against movement cheats. BattlEye focuses on process/memory integrity; the server validates physics.

### Server Config Security Settings

| Setting | Default | Purpose |
|---------|---------|---------|
| `VAC_Secure` | true | Require VAC enabled |
| `BattlEye_Secure` | true | Require BattlEye enabled |
| `Max_Ping_Milliseconds` | 750 | Kick high-ping players |
| `Max_Packets_Per_Second` | 50.0 | Rate limit per player |
| `Fake_Lag_Threshold_Seconds` | 3.0 | Lag switch detection |
| `Fake_Lag_Damage_Penalty_Multiplier` | 0.1 | Reduced PvP damage during suspected lag |
| `Enable_Kick_Input_Spam` | false | Kick for excessive inputs |
| `Validate_EconInfo_Hash` | true | Prevent visibility exploits |
| `Validate_MasterBundle_Hashes` | true | Verify asset integrity |

### Third-Party Plugins

- **UnturnedGuard** -- commercial anti-cheat with Demo/Gold tiers ([docs](https://unturnedguard.readthedocs.io/))
- **Darkness AntiCheat** (abandoned) -- noclip detection with configurable rate, anti-aim, punch distance validation, fake-lag detection ([GitHub](https://github.com/Darkness-Anti-Cheat/Darkness-AntiCheat))
- **Detect Cheats by DKay** -- fly detection module checks velocity, current collisions, and height above objects at configurable intervals. Requires 2+ consecutive detections to avoid false positives. Trees/resources do NOT count as objects (no server-side collider). ([uMod](https://umod.org/plugins/detect-cheats))

**Sources**:
- [Reversing BEDaisy.sys -- Adrian's Security Research](https://s4dbrd.github.io/posts/reversing-bedaisy/)
- [BattlEye -- Official Unturned Wiki](https://unturned.wiki.gg/wiki/BattlEye)
- [BattlEye FAQ -- Steam Discussion](https://steamcommunity.com/app/304930/discussions/0/217691032433994895/)
- [Anti-Cheat FAQ: Bans &amp; Appeals -- Steam](https://steamcommunity.com/app/304930/discussions/0/805722062315396073/)
- [ACM 2025 Paper -- Battling The Eye](https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3733817.3762701)
- [BattlEye Bypass Overview -- GuidedHacking](https://guidedhacking.com/threads/anticheat-battleye-bypass-overview.11602/)

---

## 7. 2025 ACADEMIC BATTLEYE BYPASS (Using Unturned as Target)

The 2025 ACM MATE Workshop paper "Battling The Eye" used Unturned v3.23.12 as the test target:

1. Loaded a custom unsigned kernel driver **before** BattlEye initialized. BEDaisy does not globally verify all loaded kernel modules.
2. Disabled BEDaisy's `ObRegisterCallbacks` by setting the `Enabled` field of `OB_CALLBACK_ENTRY` to `false`. The callback stays registered but its routines are never invoked.
3. Successfully injected a DLL into the Unturned process and ran an internal cheat for several minutes without detection.
4. Findings were responsibly disclosed to BattlEye; some mitigations were integrated into subsequent BattlEye versions.

---

## 8. CURRENT EXPLOIT STATUS (2024-2026)

**What works on default public BattlEye servers**:
- ESP, aimbot, wallhack -- advertised as "undetected" by multiple providers, maintained through frequent signature updates
- The academic BattlEye bypass demonstrated viability in 2025

**What does NOT reliably work on default servers**:
- **Noclip, fly hacks, speed hacks** are blocked by the default `Can_Trust_Client_Movement = false` server validation. Even if BattlEye is bypassed, the server rejects invalid movement.
- Vehicle fly is limited by `Valid_Speed_*` sanity checks (vehicles freeze/rubber-band when exceeding speed thresholds)

**What works on misconfigured servers**:
- Servers with `Can_Trust_Client_Movement = true` or `-ForceTrustClient` are fully vulnerable to all movement cheats
- This is reportedly rare on competitive/public servers

**Key architectural conclusion**: The server-side movement validation, not BattlEye, is the real barrier for movement cheats. BattlEye protects against memory-reading cheats (ESP, aimbot). DMA-based external reads bypass BattlEye entirely but still face server-side movement authority for any writes that alter player/vehicle position.

---

## SUMMARY TABLE

| System | Authority Model | Exploitable? |
|--------|----------------|-------------|
| Player movement/position | Client-authoritative, server-validated (when `Can_Trust_Client_Movement = false`) | Only if server misconfigured |
| Vehicle movement/position | **Client-authoritative** (server sanity checks on speed only) | Yes -- gravity toggle, speed within thresholds |
| Structure/barricade collision | Server-side collision meshes, client-side rendering | Door animation physics exploits |
| Vehicle-to-structure collision | Client-side (breaks at render distance) | Yes -- render distance bypass |
| Player health/inventory | Server-authoritative | No |
| Hit detection | Unclear from docs | Likely client-authoritative (aimbot works) |</result>

## Unturned Cheat Source Code Repositories Found

### 1. BoCheat-Unturned (lgv-0/BoCheat-Unturned)
**URL:** https://github.com/lgv-0/BoCheat-Unturned
**Status:** Archived Feb 2021, C# 100%
**Injection method:** Harmony 1.2.0.1 patching via `HarmonyInstance.Create("main")` + `PatchAll()`

**Source file structure (HInj/HInj/):**
- `Main.cs` -- initializer, creates persistent GameObject, attaches components
- `Aimbot.cs` -- legit aimbot with visibility checks, drop calculation, smoothing
- `RageBot.cs` -- rage aimbot with wall penetration
- `Drawing.cs` -- ESP rendering (players, items, vehicles, chams/glow/wireframe shaders)
- `Miscellaneous.cs` -- no recoil/sway, freecam, force GPS/compass, fast salvage, no fog, skin changer
- `Items.cs` -- selective item grabbing (guns, ammo, medical, food)
- `Vehicles.cs` -- vehicle flight, ping manipulation, sink
- `Menu.cs` -- ImGUI-style menu
- `Skins.cs` -- skin changer
- `Toolkit.cs` -- utility methods
- `Friend.cs` -- friend list management

**Hooks directory (Harmony patches on SDG.Unturned methods):**

**`Hooks/sendRaycast/Hold.cs`** -- patches `PlayerInput.sendRaycast(RaycastInfo, ERaycastInfoUsage)`:
```csharp
[HarmonyPatch(typeof(PlayerInput), "sendRaycast",
    new System.Type[] { typeof(RaycastInfo), typeof(ERaycastInfoUsage) })]
public class sendRaycast
{
    public static bool Prefix(ref RaycastInfo info, ref ERaycastInfoUsage usage)
    {
        if (Global.AllOff) return true;
        if ((info.zombie != null || info.player != null) &amp;&amp;
            (Global.AimSettings.ForceHit || Global.AimSettings.RageBot))
            info.limb = ELimb.SKULL;
        return true;
    }
}
```
This is the silent aim / force headshot mechanism. It intercepts the client-side raycast result before it is sent to the server, changing the hit limb to SKULL. This works because **hit detection is client-authoritative** -- the client tells the server where the bullet hit and on which limb.

**`Hooks/Ballistics/Hold.cs`** -- patches `UseableGun.ballistics`:
- Redirects bullet direction toward target player skull/body
- Conditionally removes bullet drop (`NoDrop` setting)
- Returns `false` to skip original ballistics calculation entirely
- References: `UseableGun`, `BulletInfo`, `Attachments`, `RaycastInfo`, `DamageTool`, `ResourceManager`, `ItemBarricadeAsset`, `ItemStructureAsset`

**`Hooks/Hook.cs`** -- patches `Player.askScreenshot` (anti-screenshot/spectator detection)

**Other hook directories:** `DoorAnimation`, `IsPositionUnderwater`, `Stun`, `UpdateAim`, `lvlLoaded`, `refreshDynamicMap`, `sendChat`, `updLighting`

**ESP implementation (Drawing.cs) -- key iteration patterns:**
- Players: `foreach (SteamPlayer x in Provider.clients)`
- Vehicles: `foreach (InteractableVehicle r in VehicleManager.vehicles)`
- Uses custom shaders loaded from asset bundle: wireframe, seethrough, silhouette, chams
- World-to-screen via `Camera.WorldToScreenPoint()`

### 2. EgguWare-Unturned (Coopyy/EgguWare-Unturned)
**URL:** https://github.com/Coopyy/EgguWare-Unturned
**Status:** Archived Dec 2023, C# 99.2% + ShaderLab 0.8%
**Injection method:** External loader required (not included), creates persistent GameObject via `Load.Start()`, attaches `Manager` MonoBehaviour

**`Weapons.cs` (177 lines)** -- full source retrieved:
- **No Spread:** `gun.spreadAim = 0f; gun.spreadHip = 0f;` on `ItemGunAsset`
- **No Recoil:** zeros `recoilMax_x/y`, `recoilMin_x/y` on `ItemGunAsset`

- **Flight:** sets `itemGravityMultiplier = 0` on `ItemCloudAsset`, uses WASD/Space/LCtrl for 3D movement without collision

**`Items.cs` (98 lines):**

- **Item clump detection:** groups nearby items by distance/count

**`Misc.cs`:**

- **Full bright:** `LightingManager.time = 1200U`
- **Free camera:** toggles `Player.player.look.isOrbiting` and `isTracking`

**`PlayerCam.cs` (110 lines):**

- References: `SteamPlayer`, `Provider`

**`Load.cs`:**
```csharp
public static void Start()
{
    CO = new GameObject();
    UnityEngine.Object.DontDestroyOnLoad(CO);
    CO.AddComponent&lt;Manager&gt;();
}
```
Standard Unity Mono injection pattern: create persistent GameObject, attach MonoBehaviour components.

```csharp
using SDG.Unturned;
using UnityEngine;
public class cheatload {
    public static void init() {
        cheatobj = new GameObject();
        Object.DontDestroyOnLoad(cheatobj);
        cheatobj.AddComponent&lt;cheatmanager&gt;();
    }
}
```
Minimal bootstrap -- actual cheat logic in `cheatmanager`.

## Unturned Networking Architecture and Validation

### Hit Detection: Client-Authoritative with Server Validation

Based on the GitHub issues and source code analysis, Unturned uses a **hybrid model** for combat:

1. **Client calculates bullet trajectory** -- `UseableGun.ballistics` runs on the client, computing bullet path with drop, travel time, etc.
2. **Client reports hit via RPC** -- `PlayerInput.sendRaycast(RaycastInfo, ERaycastInfoUsage)` sends the hit result (target, limb, position) to the server
3. **Server validates with line-of-sight check** -- the server performs a raycast from the shooter's position to the hit point to verify line-of-sight. If the shooter cannot see the target, the hit is rejected.
4. **Server validation threshold has been tightened** over time to reduce cheating effectiveness.

**Critical vulnerability:** Since the client tells the server WHAT it hit and WHERE (including which limb), the `sendRaycast` hook can change `info.limb = ELimb.SKULL` to force headshots. The server validates the line of sight but trusts the client's limb selection. This is why silent aim and force-headshot cheats work in multiplayer.

**Known issue with ballistics:** When ballistics are enabled on servers, bullets that travel over time can fail the server's line-of-sight check if the shooter moves behind cover between firing and impact (GitHub issue #4404, #5132). The community proposed saving historical aim positions in `UseableGun.onSpawnBullet` to reduce false positives.

### Movement: Server Validates but Client Controls

- **Movement is client-predicted** -- the client sends position updates to the server
- **Server validates movement** -- speed hack, fly hack, and teleport are flagged by native server-side validation. The server rejects invalid position data and flags the account.
- **`-ForceTrustClient` launch option** disables movement validation for vehicles, indicating vehicle movement validation exists but can be toggled
- **Speed hack via `PlayerMovement`** -- cheats modify private static fields `SPEED_STAND`, `SPEED_SPRINT`, `SPEED_PRONE`, `JUMP` via reflection. These are **client-side only** values and will be caught by server validation in multiplayer.
- **Vehicle noclip** -- disabling gravity and kinematic physics on vehicle rigidbodies works because vehicle physics are partially client-authoritative

### RPC System (Post-Rewrite)

The game moved from `SteamChannel`-based RPCs to a new system:
- **`ServerInvocable`** -- methods the client can call on the server
- **`ClientInvocable`** -- methods the server can call on the client
- **`ServerInvocationContext`** / **`ClientInvocationContext`** replace the old `CSteamID senderId` parameter
- **`NetId`** replaces `SteamChannel.id`
- Auto-generated `_NetMethods` classes handle read/write serialization

### Anti-Cheat Layers

1. **BattlEye** -- signature scanning for known cheat binaries, updated server-side without game patches. "BattlEye's Unturned-specific anti-cheat code is still private (even we don't have access to it)" -- Nelson/SDG
2. **Native server-side validation** -- flags speed hacks, fly hacks, item spawners, invalid hit data
3. **Behavioral heuristics** -- aim velocity curves, mouse movement pattern analysis
4. **Admin tools** -- spectator mode, teleport-to-player, screenshot capture (`Player.askScreenshot`)
5. **VAC** -- optional Valve Anti-Cheat on VAC-secured servers

### What Works in Multiplayer vs. Single-Player

| Feature | Single-Player | Multiplayer |
|---------|:---:|:---:|
| ESP (player/item/vehicle rendering) | Yes | **Yes** -- purely client-side visual, no server interaction |
| No Recoil / No Spread / No Sway | Yes | **Yes** -- modifies client-side asset values only |
| Silent Aim / Force Headshot | Yes | **Yes** -- client reports hit data, server trusts limb selection |
| Aimbot (visual snap) | Yes | **Yes** -- client-side camera/aim control |
| Speed Hack (PlayerMovement fields) | Yes | **Caught** -- server validates movement speed |
| Flight / Noclip (player) | Yes | **Caught** -- server validates position |
| Vehicle Noclip | Yes | **Partially works** -- vehicle physics are partially client-authoritative |
| God Mode (health modification) | Yes | **No** -- health is server-authoritative |
| Item Spawning | Yes | **No** -- server-authoritative |
| Rapid Fire / No Reload | Yes | **Partially** -- client can fire faster but server validates ammo state |
| Freecam | Yes | **Yes** -- client-side camera only |
| Full Bright | Yes | **Yes** -- client-side lighting only |
| Anti-Screenshot (askScreenshot hook) | N/A | **Yes** -- intercepts server screenshot requests |

### Key SDG.Unturned Classes Used by Cheats

- **`Provider`** -- `Provider.clients` (list of `SteamPlayer`), `Provider.isConnected`, `Provider.isLoading`
- **`Player`** -- `Player.player` (local player singleton), `.equipment`, `.movement`, `.look`, `.animator`, `.life`
- **`SteamPlayer`** -- represents each connected player, iterated for ESP
- **`PlayerMovement`** -- private static fields `SPEED_STAND`, `SPEED_SPRINT`, `SPEED_PRONE`, `JUMP`; instance field `pluginSpeedMultiplier`, `pluginJumpMultiplier`, `pluginGravityMultiplier`
- **`PlayerLife`** -- `.isDead`, health management
- **`PlayerInput`** -- `sendRaycast(RaycastInfo, ERaycastInfoUsage)` -- the critical RPC for hit reporting
- **`PlayerLook`** -- `.aim`, `.isOrbiting`, `.isTracking`
- **`PlayerEquipment`** -- `.asset`, `.useable`, `.isBusy`
- **`PlayerAnimator`** -- `.viewSway`
- **`ItemGunAsset`** -- `.spreadAim`, `.spreadHip`, `.recoilMax_x/y`, `.recoilMin_x/y`, `.playerDamageMultiplier`
- **`UseableGun`** -- `.ballistics()`, private fields `isHammering`, `needsRechamber`, `reloadTime`; method `updateCrosshair`
- **`RaycastInfo`** -- `.player`, `.zombie`, `.limb`, `.point`, `.dir`
- **`ELimb`** -- `SKULL`, etc.
- **`DamageTool`** -- `getPlayerArmor(ELimb, Player)`
- **`InteractableItem`** -- `.use()` for auto-pickup
- **`InteractableVehicle`** -- vehicle physics manipulation
- **`VehicleManager`** -- `.vehicles` list
- **`LightingManager`** -- `.time` for fullbright
- **`ChatManager`** -- `.instance.channel.send()` for chat spam
- **`AnimalManager`** -- `sendAnimalAttack` (Harmony-patched in plugin examples)
- **`ItemCloudAsset`** -- `.itemGravityMultiplier` for flight

### Injection Methods Used

1. **Mono injection** -- standard Unity Mono DLL injection using tools like SharpMono Injector, GH Mono Injector, MonoJunkie, MonoJabber. Creates a persistent `GameObject` with `DontDestroyOnLoad` and attaches `MonoBehaviour` components.
2. **Harmony patching** -- runtime method patching of SDG.Unturned methods using `[HarmonyPatch]` attributes with Prefix/Postfix methods. Used for intercepting `sendRaycast`, `ballistics`, `askScreenshot`, etc.
3. **IL injection via dnSpy** -- direct modification of game DLLs (e.g., `System.Core.dll`) to insert static constructor calls into the cheat DLL.
4. **Reflection** -- extensive use of `GetField`/`GetMethod` with `BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance` to access private fields like `isHammering`, `needsRechamber`, `reloadTime`, and private static fields like `SPEED_STAND`.
5. **External overlay (Direct2D)** -- stream-proof rendering that draws ESP outside the game process.

### Harmony Patching Documentation (from Restore Monarchy)

The Unturned plugin ecosystem itself uses Harmony officially:
```csharp
[HarmonyPatch(typeof(AnimalManager))]
class AnimalManagerPatches
{
    [HarmonyPatch("sendAnimalAttack")]
    [HarmonyPrefix]
    static bool PrefixAttack(Animal animal)
    {
        return false; // Skips original method
    }
}
```
Setup via `RocketPlugin` with `Harmony.PatchAll()` in `Load()` and `UnpatchAll()` in `Unload()`. Developers use dnSpy to discover patchable methods.

## 1. ADMIN STATUS: SERVER-SIDE VALIDATED

### How isAdmin Works Internally

From the datamined `SteamPlayer.cs`, admin status is stored as a private boolean:

```csharp
private bool _isAdmin;

public bool isAdmin
{
    get
    {
        if (OptionsSettings.ShouldAnonymizeMultiplayerDetails &amp;&amp; 
            playerID.steamID != Provider.user)
            return false;
        return _isAdmin;
    }
    set { _isAdmin = value; }
}
```

The property has a public setter, meaning **in-process code can write to it**. However, this is only meaningful locally -- the server determines admin status through `SteamAdminlist`.

### SteamAdminlist: Server-Side Authority

From the datamined `SteamAdminlist.cs`, admin verification is purely server-side:

```csharp
public static bool checkAdmin(CSteamID playerID)
{
    if (playerID == ownerID) return true;
    for (int i = 0; i &lt; list.Count; i++)
    {
        if (list[i].playerID == playerID) return true;
    }
    return false;
}
```

- Admins are loaded from `/Server/Adminlist.dat` on the **server**
- The owner ID (from `Commands.dat`) is always admin
- Admin status changes broadcast `EClientMessage.Admined` / `EClientMessage.Unadmined` to clients
- **There is no client-to-server RPC to request admin status** -- the server sets it during player join based on `SteamAdminlist.checkAdmin()`

### Command Execution Validation

From the datamined `ChatManager.cs`, admin commands are validated server-side:

```csharp
[SteamCall(ESteamCallValidation.SERVERSIDE, ratelimitHz = 15, legacyName = "askChat")]
public static void ReceiveChatRequest(in ServerInvocationContext context, byte flags, string text)
```

The command processing flow in `process()`:

```csharp
string text = cmd.Substring(0, 1);
if (text == "@" || text == "/")
{
    if (!Dedicator.IsDedicatedServer || player.isAdmin)
    {
        shouldExecuteCommand = true;
    }
}
```

The server checks `player.isAdmin` (which was set by the server from `SteamAdminlist`) before passing to `Commander.execute()`. The `ESteamCallValidation.SERVERSIDE` attribute ensures only the server framework can invoke this handler.

### Can Admin Be Spoofed?

**Setting `isAdmin = true` on the client is cosmetic only.** The server:
1. Sets `isAdmin` during player construction based on `SteamAdminlist.checkAdmin()`
2. Validates admin status server-side before executing any admin command
3. Uses `ServerInvocationContext` to identify the calling player (cannot be forged)
4. Rate-limits command requests to 15Hz

Setting the local `_isAdmin` field via memory editing or Harmony patching would only change the local client's perception (e.g., showing admin UI), but the server would reject any admin command attempts because its own copy of the player's admin status would still be `false`.

---

## 2. DAMAGE: SERVER-AUTHORITATIVE WITH CLIENT RAYCASTS

### The Hit Registration Flow

The complete damage flow, reconstructed from datamined source:

**Step 1 -- Client performs raycast** (from `UseableGun.cs`):
```csharp
RaycastInfo raycastInfo = DamageTool.raycast(
    new Ray(base.player.look.aim.position, forward), 
    512f, RayMasks.DAMAGE_CLIENT, base.player);
if (raycastInfo.transform != null)
{
    base.player.input.sendRaycast(raycastInfo, ERaycastInfoUsage.Gun);
}
```

**Step 2 -- Client sends raycast data** (from `PlayerInput.cs`):
The client sends: target references (player/zombie/animal/vehicle), impact point, direction, normal, limb hit, material, and transform references. Critically, the client does NOT send a damage value.

**Step 3 -- Server validates the raycast** (from `PlayerInput.cs`):
```csharp
// Two-stage occlusion check:
// Forward raycast from aim position to target
Physics.Raycast(new Ray(vector, vector3), out obstruction, 
    magnitude - 0.025f, RayMasks.DAMAGE_SERVER);

// Reverse raycast from impact point backward
Physics.Raycast(new Ray(inputInfo.point + vector3 * 0.025f, -vector3), 
    out obstruction, magnitude - 0.025f, RayMasks.DAMAGE_SERVER);
```

The server validates:
- Line-of-sight between aim position and target (obstruction check)
- Reverse raycast validation
- Accepts hits on "Ground" or "Environment" tags
- Validates target ownership via `IsChildOf()` check

**Step 4 -- Server calculates damage** (from `DamageTool.cs`):
```csharp
public static void damagePlayer(DamagePlayerParameters parameters, out EPlayerKill kill)
{
    int num = Mathf.FloorToInt(parameters.damage * parameters.times);
    byte b = (byte)Mathf.Min(255, num);
    // Armor applied server-side:
    parameters.times *= getPlayerArmor(parameters.limb, parameters.player);
}
```

**Step 5 -- Server applies damage** (from `PlayerLife.cs`):
```csharp
public void askDamage(byte amount, Vector3 newRagdoll, EDeathCause newCause, 
    ELimb newLimb, CSteamID newKiller, out EPlayerKill kill, ...)
```

Damage is capped at 255, floored, and armor is applied entirely server-side.

### What the Client Controls vs What the Server Controls

| Aspect | Authority | Details |
|--------|-----------|---------|
| Raycast origin/direction | Client | Client sends where they aimed |
| Hit target selection | Client | Client says who/what was hit |
| Hit limb | Client | Client says which limb was hit |
| Raycast validation | Server | Server does LOS check with DAMAGE_SERVER mask |
| Damage value | Server | Calculated from weapon asset data, quality, armor |
| Armor calculation | Server | `getPlayerArmor()` runs server-side |
| Health deduction | Server | `askDamage()` modifies health server-side |

### Anti-Cheat Measures in the Input System

From `PlayerInput.cs`:

1. **Fake lag detection**: If no input for &gt; `Fake_Lag_Threshold_Seconds`, damage multiplier drops to `0.1f`
2. **Input rate limiting**: `MAX_ASKINPUT_PER_SECOND` (soft) and `KICK_ASKINPUT_PER_SECOND` (hard kick)
3. **Input timeout**: 20-second timeout kicks idle/frozen clients
4. **Occlusion validation**: Dual-direction raycast check on every hit

### Can Damage Be Modified by Cheats?

**The client cannot directly set damage values.** However, the client does control:

1. **Which limb is reported as hit** -- a cheat could always report headshots (`ELimb.SKULL`) even when hitting legs, getting the headshot multiplier. The server validates LOS but not which specific limb the bullet struck.

2. **Silent aim / aim manipulation** -- since the client performs the initial raycast and sends results, a cheat can manipulate aim direction to always hit targets. The server validates LOS from the player's position but trusts the client's aim vector.

3. **No recoil / no spread** -- these are client-side visual/mechanical calculations. The client sends the final aim direction after recoil/spread would have been applied, so removing them is purely local and undetectable by the server alone (BattlEye would need to detect the modification).

4. **Weapon modifications (no sway, instant reload)** -- some are client-authoritative because the client manages animation states locally.

**What the client CANNOT do:**
- Send arbitrary damage values (server calculates from weapon stats)
- Bypass armor calculations (server-side)
- Damage players through walls (server LOS validation)
- Deal damage with no ammo (server validates ammo count)

---

## 3. MOVEMENT VALIDATION

From the official documentation, `-ForceTrustClient` disables vehicle movement validation. The documentation explicitly warns: "Using this is not recommended! It is easier for cheaters to fly cars with movement limits disabled."

The EgguWare cheat source lists "movement verification bypass including noclip, speed multiplication, and jump height adjustment" as a feature, confirming that movement validation exists but has been bypassed in practice. The developers have acknowledged that vehicle movement is still client-authoritative in some respects and plan to make it fully server-authoritative.

---

## 4. BATTLEYE INTEGRATION

- BattlEye is **only active on BattlEye-secured servers** -- non-BE servers have no anti-cheat beyond Unturned's built-in validation
- As of November 2022, BattlEye issues permanent bans for lag switch exploits
- Internal cheats (Harmony-based C# injection) can bypass BattlEye using various DLL injection techniques
- The DeftHack README explicitly notes it is "only suitable for Non-BE and Non-VAC servers" without a separate bypass
- Academic research (ACM 2025) has studied BattlEye's detection mechanisms, including DLL injection tests against Unturned specifically

---

## 5. EXISTING CHEAT FEATURES (From Open-Source Cheats)

From EgguWare and its forks (BoCheat, DeftHack, Fixed-EgguWare):

**Client-side exploits that work (bypass server validation):**
- Silent aim / aimlock (client controls raycast direction)
- No recoil / no spread / no sway (client-side weapon mechanics)
- ESP / glow / wallhack (client-side rendering)
- Speed hack / noclip (partially server-validated -- may get rejected on well-configured servers)
- Vehicle fly / teleport (depends on ForceTrustClient setting)

**Features that do NOT include admin spoof or damage multiplier:**
None of the open-source cheats (EgguWare, BoCheat, DeftHack, Fixed-EgguWare) list admin spoofing or direct damage modification as features. This is consistent with the server-authoritative architecture -- these would be rejected by the server.

---

## 6. SUMMARY CONCLUSIONS

| Feature | Server Validated? | Exploitable? |
|---------|------------------|-------------|
| Admin status | Yes -- `SteamAdminlist.checkAdmin()` on server | No -- setting local `isAdmin` is cosmetic only |
| Admin commands | Yes -- `ChatManager.ReceiveChatRequest` checks `player.isAdmin` server-side | No -- server rejects commands from non-admins |
| Damage values | Yes -- calculated from weapon asset data server-side | No -- client cannot send arbitrary damage |
| Hit target/limb | Partially -- server validates LOS but trusts client's limb selection | Yes -- limb spoofing for headshot multipliers |
| Aim direction | Client-authoritative | Yes -- silent aim works |
| Recoil/spread | Client-side | Yes -- removal is undetectable by server |
| Movement | Partially validated (configurable) | Depends on server config |
| Vehicle movement | Client-authoritative (with optional validation) | Yes -- unless server enables validation |

### Claude

Got it — Unturned admin spoof confirmed impossible in multiplayer (server checks `SteamAdminlist.checkAdmin()` independently, setting local `isAdmin` is cosmetic only). Damage is server-calculated from weapon stats, but limb spoofing for forced headshots works since the server trusts the client's reported limb. This aligns with the matrix entry.

## UNTURNED SERVER-SIDE VALIDATION -- COMPREHENSIVE TECHNICAL REPORT

---

### 1. MOVEMENT / SPEED -- Server-Validated (Auth Physics)

**Architecture:** Unturned uses client-side prediction with server reconciliation. The client simulates movement locally and sends input packets to the server. The server re-simulates using the client's inputs and compares the resulting position.

**How validation works (from datamined `PlayerMovement.cs`):**
- Fixed simulation rate: `RATE = 0.08f` (12.5 Hz client input send rate)
- Server processes at `TOCK_PER_SECOND = 50` ticks/sec
- Client sends `WalkingPlayerInputPacket` containing `analog` (movement vector), `clientPosition`, `pitch`, `yaw`, `keys`
- Server runs `player.movement.simulate()` with the client's inputs
- Position mismatch threshold: `sqrMagnitude &gt; 0.0004f` (0.02 units). On mismatch, server sends correction via `ReceiveSimulateMispredictedInputs()`
- Speed is calculated as: `totalSpeedMultiplier = pluginSpeedMultiplier * clothing.movementSpeedMultiplier * equipment.equipableMovementSpeedMultiplier`

**Anti-cheat config parameters:**
- `Max_Walk_Speed`: **15** (speed ceiling for character movement validation)
- `Max_Flagged_Packets_Before_Kick`: **30**
- `Max_Invalid_Character_Packets_Before_Kick`: **20**

**Key update (3.21.31.0):** Overhauled movement input in multiplayer. When positions don't match, the client re-simulates unacknowledged inputs (rewind+replay) to eliminate rubberbanding. This replaced the old hard-snap behavior.

**Legacy toggle:** Auth physics could previously be disabled with `misc.dont_enable_this true`. The modern server flag `-ForceTrustClient` only affects **vehicle** movement validation, not on-foot player movement. Character auth physics can no longer be easily disabled.

**Practical implications:** Pure speed multiplication triggers server correction/rubberbanding. However, multiple cheat vendors still advertise speed modification as working, suggesting either (a) they operate within the validation threshold, (b) they manipulate tick timing/input rate to fool the delta check, or (c) the `Max_Walk_Speed = 15` ceiling is generous enough to allow modest boosts.

**Sources:**
- https://docs.smartlydressedgames.com/en/stable/about/launch-options.html
- https://steamcommunity.com/app/304930/discussions/0/1692669912393791476/
- https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining (PlayerMovement.cs, PlayerInput.cs)
- https://docs.wasabihosting.com/games/unturned/server-configuration
- https://store.steampowered.com/news/app/304930/view/4319509904905356205
- https://unturned.wiki.gg/wiki/Gameplay_config

---

### 2. TELEPORTATION -- Server-Detected with Explicit Counters

**Detection mechanism:**
- `tellState()` in `PlayerMovement.cs` checks `(newPosition - lastUpdatePos).sqrMagnitude &gt; 256f` (distance jump &gt; 16 units)
- The server explicitly tracks teleport events as a distinct category

**Anti-cheat config parameters:**
- `Max_Successful_Teleports_Before_Kick`: **5** -- successful (server-accepted) large position jumps before auto-kick
- `Max_Unsuccessful_Teleports_Before_Kick`: **5** -- failed/rejected teleport attempts before auto-kick

After 5 teleport-like position discontinuities (in either category), the player is automatically kicked. This means the server DOES detect teleportation as a separate event type from general speed violations.

**However, from the datamined source:** When a teleport-threshold jump is detected, it **resets the snapshot** rather than outright rejecting the position. Large jumps are accommodated to some degree before the counter triggers a kick. This means a cheat could potentially execute 4 teleports before being kicked.

**Vehicle teleportation:**
- `Max_Vehicle_Distance_Before_Kick`: **60** (much more permissive than character -- 60 units vs ~16 units)
- `Max_Invalid_Vehicle_Packets_Before_Kick`: **10**

**Modding API confirmation:** The OpenMod API exposes `UnturnedPlayerTeleportingEvent` -- a cancellable event with Position/Yaw properties, confirming the server tracks teleportation as a discrete internal event.

**Sources:**
- https://docs.wasabihosting.com/games/unturned/server-configuration
- https://docs.wasabihosting.com/games/unturned/server-optimization
- https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining (PlayerMovement.cs)
- https://openmod.github.io/openmod-docs/api/OpenMod.Unturned.Players.Movement.Events.UnturnedPlayerTeleportingEvent.html

---

### 3. NO-CLIP / FLY -- NOT Validated Server-Side

**No evidence of server-side collision checks.** The server validates position deltas against speed limits but does NOT verify collision with world geometry or enforce gravity state.

- `checkGround()` uses a sphere-cast for ground detection but is overridable -- `controller.isGrounded` forces grounded state
- Vehicle/climbing/swimming states automatically set `_isGrounded = true` without validation
- No per-tick path validity check or physics-state enforcement exists

**Community anti-cheat plugins tried to fill this gap:** The Darkness AntiCheat plugin (now abandoned) listed noclip detection as a feature, with `noclip_detection_rate: 3` (3 violations before action). The fact that a third-party plugin attempted this confirms the native game does not reliably detect noclip.

**Practical:** If noclip movement stays within the `Max_Walk_Speed` bounds (speed-wise), the position deltas will pass validation. Fly hack similarly -- if Y-axis movement stays within speed bounds, the server cannot distinguish flying from legitimate vertical movement (ladders, falling).

**Sources:**
- https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining (PlayerMovement.cs)
- https://github.com/Darkness-Anti-Cheat/Darkness-AntiCheat
- https://github.com/ShimmyMySherbet/ShimmysAdminTools

---

### 4. RECOIL -- Entirely Client-Side, No Server Validation

**Recoil is a purely visual/camera effect applied client-side.** The server does not track or validate the player's camera recoil state.

**How recoil works:**
- When firing, the client rotates the player's camera by a random amount within configured min/max range
- Gun model itself remains static -- only the screen/camera moves
- Defined per-weapon via asset parameters:
  - `Recoil_Min_X` / `Recoil_Max_X` -- horizontal camera rotation in degrees
  - `Recoil_Min_Y` / `Recoil_Max_Y` -- vertical camera rotation in degrees
  - Situational multipliers: `Recoil_Crouch` (0.85), `Recoil_Prone` (0.7), `Recoil_Sprint` (1.25), `Recoil_Midair` (1.0), `Recoil_Swimming` (1.1)
  - Recovery: `Recover_X` / `Recover_Y` -- counter-animation over 250ms
  - `Aiming_Recoil_Multiplier` (default 1.0)

**Can no-recoil cheats work?** Yes, and they are described as "basically undetectable as admins can't spy and see recoil" (Steam community discussion). Zeroing out recoil values in memory does not cause any server-side rejection because the server never receives or validates recoil data.

**Sources:**
- https://docs.smartlydressedgames.com/en/latest/items/gun-asset.html
- https://steamcommunity.com/app/304930/discussions/0/1678063648173293941/
- https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining (UseableGun.cs)

---

### 5. SPREAD -- Client-Side, Client-Authoritative, No Server Validation

**Spread determines the angular deviation of a bullet from the crosshair center, and it is calculated entirely on the client.**

**How spread works:**
- `CalculateSpreadAngleRadians()` runs client-side with `GetSimulationAimAlpha()`
- Incorporates weapon quality, aiming state, attachment effects, stance multipliers
- Base angle: `Spread_Angle_Degrees` (e.g., 15 means bullet can deviate up to 15 degrees off-center; 0 = perfect accuracy)
- Multipliers: `Spread_Aim` (often 0 when ADS = perfect accuracy), `Spread_Crouch` (0.85), `Spread_Prone` (0.7), `Spread_Sprint` (1.25), `Spread_Midair` (1.5), `Spread_Swimming` (1.1)

**Critical architectural finding (GitHub issue #4450):** "Bullets are sent with a fixed direction (the player's forward) and a starting position. As the player sends more inputs, the position is increased along the direction clientside, but not serverside." The server does NOT independently roll its own spread value or verify the client's spread calculation.

**Can no-spread cheats work?** Yes. The client determines bullet direction (including spread), so a cheat can send bullets with direction exactly matching crosshair center, eliminating all randomness. Multiple cheat sources confirm this.

**Sources:**
- https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/4450
- https://docs.smartlydressedgames.com/en/latest/items/gun-asset.html
- https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining (UseableGun.cs)

---

### 6. BULLET TRAJECTORY / HIT REGISTRATION -- Client-Authoritative with Weak Server LOS Check

**Bullet simulation is client-authoritative. The server performs only a line-of-sight plausibility check.**

**How it works:**
1. Client fires -- determines bullet start position and direction (including spread)
2. Client simulates bullet travel (ballistic steps, drop, gravity)
3. Client performs the raycast hit detection locally
4. Client reports the hit to the server via `player.input.sendRaycast(raycastInfo, ERaycastInfoUsage.Gun)`
5. Server performs a **line-of-sight check** from shooter's position to impact point
6. If LOS passes, server accepts the hit and applies damage

**What the server tracks:** Bullet steps (time progression/lifespan). What it does NOT track: bullet position coordinates, bullet direction vector, trajectory path.

**The vulnerability (issue #4450):** "By updating on every tock, the position and direction of the bullet clientside to match with the client's current transform, bullets sent seconds ago can land at exactly where the client is looking at that exact moment, as if ballistics were disabled on the server."

**Server-side hit validation improvements (3.25.x patches):**
- **3.25.1.0**: Removed once-per-input-frame limit on server-side LOS checks, better protecting against bullet hit spoofing (higher server CPU)
- **3.25.7.0**: "Significantly tightened server hit validation threshold"
- **3.25.8.0**: Fixed server-side bullet LOS test using too-recent origin point. Tightened barricade hit validation bounds.

**Known issue (issue #4404):** "If the player does not have line of sight with where a bullet impacts on an entity, then the bullet does not register." When ballistics are active and the player moves between firing and impact, legitimate hits can fail the LOS check (false positives).

**Sources:**
- https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/4450
- https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/4404
- https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/5132
- https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/1358
- https://unturned-servers.net/blog/438/32570-update-notes/
- https://unturned-servers.net/blog/426/screenshot-contest-2025/
- https://unturned-servers.net/blog/441/32580-update-notes/

---

### 7. ASSET INTEGRITY (Why File Modification Fails in Multiplayer)

SDG added a general asset integrity verification system. When equipping items in multiplayer, the server verifies gun configuration matches. Originally case-by-case checks (recoil, spread, vehicles, attachments), now replaced with a universal solution: "the integrity of all assets used by the level and server is verified in multiplayer, as well as the Unity asset bundles they loaded from."

**Result:** Modifying .dat files (zeroing recoil/spread) only works in singleplayer. In multiplayer, mismatched asset configurations trigger: "Client and server disagree on asset [...] configuration."

**However:** This does NOT prevent runtime memory/code patching (internal cheats). The asset integrity check validates files on disk, not in-memory values during gameplay.

- `Validate_EconInfo_Hash`: **true** (default) -- prevents client-side economy exploits
- `Validate_MasterBundle_Hashes`: **true** (default) -- verifies asset bundle integrity on connection

**Sources:**
- https://support.smartlydressedgames.com/hc/en-us/articles/20501325473812
- https://unturned.wiki.gg/wiki/Gameplay_config

---

### 8. LAG SWITCH / FAKE LAG DETECTION

- `Fake_Lag_Threshold_Seconds`: **3.0** -- if gap between input packets exceeds this, client is flagged
- `Fake_Lag_Damage_Penalty_Multiplier`: **0.1** -- reduces flagged player's damage to 10% (PvP only)
- `Fake_Lag_Log_Warnings`: **false** (default)
- `Max_Packets_Per_Second`: **50.0**
- `Rate_Limit_Kick_Threshold`: **10** -- kicks after N rate limit violations
- `Max_Ping_Milliseconds`: **750** (Anti_Cheat section: `Max_Ping: 1250`)

**BattlEye lag switch bans:** Since November 2022, BattlEye permanently bans for lag switch usage.

**Sources:**
- https://restoremonarchy.com/blog/ush05-config-json-explained-server-section
- https://unturned.wiki.gg/wiki/BattlEye

---

### 9. ANTI-CHEAT LAYERS (3-Tier System)

1. **Built-in server-side validation (auth physics):** Position delta vs speed checks, teleport counting, LOS hit validation, packet validation, lag-switch detection, asset hash verification. Active always.

2. **BattlEye** (default: enabled): Kernel-mode driver (`BEDaisy.sys`), signature scanning, memory scanning, DLL injection blocking, aim velocity pattern monitoring. Updates server-side without game patches. HWID bans cross all BattlEye titles (DayZ, Tarkov, R6S, etc.).

3. **VAC** (default: enabled): Standard Valve Anti-Cheat signature detection.

**Sources:**
- https://unturned.wiki.gg/wiki/BattlEye
- https://unturned.wiki.gg/wiki/Valve_Anti-Cheat
- https://www.battleye.com/

---

### 10. KNOWN CHEAT FEATURES AND WHAT WORKS

**From EgguWare (open-source, outdated but architecturally informative):**

| Feature | Category | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| No Recoil | Weapon | Works -- client-side only, server never validates |
| No Spread | Weapon | Works -- client determines bullet direction |
| No Sway | Weapon | Works -- visual only |
| Silent Aim | Weapon | Works -- client-authoritative hit registration |
| Player Noclip | Movement | Works if speed stays within threshold |
| Player Run Speed | Movement | Works within validation bounds |
| Player Jump Height | Movement | Works within validation bounds |
| Vehicle Noclip | Movement | Easier -- vehicles are client-authoritative |
| Instant Reload | Weapon | Likely detectable -- server tracks ammo state |
| Free Camera | Visual | Works -- camera is client-side |

**Implementation methods:**
- **Internal (C# / Harmony):** Patches .NET/Mono methods at runtime. No-recoil patches the camera rotation function. No-spread overrides bullet direction to match crosshair. Common approach since Unturned is Unity/C#.
- **External:** Sets local admin flag via memory write, enabling freecam + in-game ESP. Also patches recoil/spread values externally.
- **Assembly-CSharp.dll replacement:** The game's core managed DLL can be decompiled, modified (navigate to `GunStats.getRecoil_Y` etc.), and replaced. Only works in singleplayer/non-VAC servers due to asset hash verification.

**Sources:**
- https://github.com/Coopyy/EgguWare-Unturned/blob/master/README.md
- https://github.com/Coopyy/Unturned-External-Base
- https://github.com/u16rogue/EgguWare-Unturned-Plus
- https://www.mpgh.net/forum/showthread.php?t=841892
- https://guidedhacking.com/threads/internal-c-unturned-cheats.18139/

---

### 11. NETWORKING FRAMEWORK

- Unturned 3.x uses a **custom networking layer built on Steamworks** (Steam Networking Sockets), NOT Unity's UNet
- RPC system: `ClientInstanceMethod` / `ServerInstanceMethod` patterns with `SteamCall` attributes
- Input packet types: `WalkingPlayerInputPacket` (on foot), `DrivingPlayerInputPacket` (vehicles)
- All packets sent with `ENetReliability.Reliable`
- Networking code generated and stored in `Assets/Runtime/Assembly-CSharp/NetGen`

**Sources:**
- https://docs.smartlydressedgames.com/en/stable/sdg/unity-project.html
- https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining

| Mechanic | Server-Validated? | Client-Authoritative? | Cheat Works? |
|---|---|---|---|
| Movement speed | Yes (position delta vs speed per tick) | Partially (client predicts, server checks) | Within threshold -- yes |
| Teleportation | Yes (&gt;16 unit jumps counted, kick after 5) | No | Limited to 4 jumps before kick |
| Noclip/fly | No (no collision/gravity validation) | Yes | Yes, if speed stays in bounds |
| Recoil | No validation at all | Fully client-side | Yes, undetectable |
| Spread | No validation at all | Fully client-side | Yes, undetectable |
| Bullet trajectory | Weak (LOS check only, no position tracking) | Client calculates and reports hits | Exploitable |
| Hit registration | Weak (LOS raycast from shooter pos) | Client performs raycast, reports to server | Exploitable |
| Vehicle movement | Minimal (client-authoritative by design) | Yes | Easily exploitable |
| Asset files | Hash verification on connection | No (server validates) | Blocked in multiplayer |
| Runtime memory | Not validated (BattlEye signature scan only) | N/A | Works until BattlEye detects signature |</result>

<result>I have gathered extensive data. Let me compile everything into a comprehensive report.

Here is a comprehensive compilation of all findings on Unturned's packet structure and networking protocol, drawn from the Unturned-Datamining repository, official SmartlyDressedGames GitHub issues, blog posts, and community sources.

---

## 1. Network Architecture Overview

Unturned (3.x) is a Unity C# game. Its networking is built on Valve's Steamworks API, using UDP. The game uses two consecutive UDP ports (default 27015 and 27016). The architecture has evolved through two major generations:

**Legacy system** (still partially present, all marked `[Obsolete]`): `ESteamPacket` + `ESteamCall` + `SteamPacker` + `SteamChannel` + `Block`

**Current system**: `EClientMessage` + `EServerMessage` + `NetPakWriter`/`NetPakReader` + `NetMessages` + `ENetReliability` + auto-generated `_NetMethods` classes

Three transport layer implementations exist:
- **SteamNetworkingSockets** (current default, ISteamNetworkingSockets API)
- **SteamNetworking** (old default, deprecated ISteamNetworking API, use `-NetTransport=SteamNetworking`)
- **SystemSockets** (Berkeley sockets TCP, use `-NetTransport=SystemSockets`)

Source: [PSA: NetTransport Rewrite - Issue #1971](https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/1971)

---

## 2. ESteamPacket Enum (Legacy, All Obsolete)

From the Unturned-Datamining repo (`Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/ESteamPacket.cs`):

```
Value 0:  UPDATE_RELIABLE_BUFFER
Value 1:  UPDATE_UNRELIABLE_BUFFER
Value 2:  UPDATE_RELIABLE_CHUNK_BUFFER
Value 3:  UPDATE_UNRELIABLE_CHUNK_BUFFER
Value 4:  UPDATE_VOICE
Value 5:  SHUTDOWN
Value 6:  WORKSHOP
Value 7:  CONNECT
Value 8:  VERIFY
Value 9:  AUTHENTICATE
Value 10: REJECTED
Value 11: ACCEPTED
Value 12: ADMINED
Value 13: UNADMINED
Value 14: BANNED
Value 15: KICKED
Value 16: CONNECTED
Value 17: DISCONNECTED
Value 18: PING_REQUEST
Value 19: PING_RESPONSE
Value 20: UPDATE_RELIABLE_INSTANT     ("Unused and will kick sender.")
Value 21: UPDATE_UNRELIABLE_INSTANT   ("Unused and will kick sender.")
Value 22: UPDATE_RELIABLE_CHUNK_INSTANT   ("Unused and will kick sender.")
Value 23: UPDATE_UNRELIABLE_CHUNK_INSTANT ("Unused and will kick sender.")
Value 24: THIRDPARTYAC
Value 25: GUIDTABLE
Value 26: CLIENT_PENDING
Value 27: MAX
```

Source: [Unturned-Datamining ESteamPacket.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/ESteamPacket.cs)

## 5. NetMessages -- Packet Format and Routing

From `Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/NetMessages.cs`:

**Header structure**: Every message begins with the message type enum value written via `WriteEnum()`, followed by message-specific payload.

**Send methods**:
| Method | Direction | Parameters |
|--------|-----------|------------|
| `SendMessageToClient()` | Server to Client | ENetReliability, single ITransportConnection |
| `SendMessageToClients()` | Server to Clients | ENetReliability, List of connections |
| `SendMessageToServer()` | Client to Server | ENetReliability, via IClientTransport |

**Receive methods**:
| Method | Side | Flow |
|--------|------|------|
| `ReceiveMessageFromClient()` | Server | ITransportConnection + NetPakReader |
| `ReceiveMessageFromServer()` | Client | processes server packets |

**Handler registration**: Two arrays indexed by message type:
- `clientReadCallbacks[]` -- 18 entries for client-side handlers
- `serverReadCallbacks[]` -- 9 entries for server-side handlers

Handler delegate signature: `delegate void ServerReadHandler(ITransportConnection, NetPakReader)`

**Legacy compatibility**: Message indices 0-1 (`UPDATE_RELIABLE_BUFFER`, `UPDATE_UNRELIABLE_BUFFER`) trigger legacy `SteamChannel` packet handling via `Provider.legacyReceiveServer()` / `SteamChannel.receive()`.

**Error handling**: Connection validation refuses garbage connections; exceptions logged with message type and connection context. Debug logging via `shouldLogBadMessages` command-line flag.

Source: [Unturned-Datamining NetMessages.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/NetMessages.cs)

---

## 6. ESteamCall Enum (Legacy)

From `Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/ESteamCall.cs`:

```
Value 0: SERVER    -- Send to server only
Value 1: ALL       -- Send to all (server + all clients)
Value 2: OTHERS    -- Send to all except sender
Value 3: OWNER     -- Send to object owner only
Value 4: NOT_OWNER -- Send to everyone except owner
Value 5: CLIENTS   -- Send to all clients
Value 6: PEERS     -- Send to peers
```

All marked `[Obsolete]`.

Source: [Unturned-Datamining ESteamCall.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/ESteamCall.cs)

```

SERVERSIDE       -- Can only run on server

```

Source: [Unturned-Datamining ESteamCallValidation.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/ESteamCallValidation.cs)

## 10. SteamChannel -- Legacy Packet Structure

From `Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/SteamChannel.cs`:

**Header constants**:
- `CHANNEL_ID_HEADER_SIZE = 1` byte
- `RPC_HEADER_SIZE = 2` bytes
- Minimum valid packet: **3 bytes**

**Wire format for legacy RPC packets** (indices 0-1 in both enums):
```
Byte 0: packet type (ESteamPacket value as byte)
Byte 1: method index (which RPC method on the target component)
Byte 2: channel ID (identifies the player/component, recycled, always &lt; 255)
Byte 3+: payload (serialized via SteamPacker/Block)
```

**Send method overloads** (all obsolete):
- `send(string name, CSteamID steamID, ESteamPacket type, params object[] arguments)` -- direct target
- `send(ESteamCall mode, ESteamPacket type, int size, byte[] packet)` -- broadcast
- `send(ESteamCall mode, byte bound, ESteamPacket type, int size, byte[] packet)` -- region-bound
- `send(ESteamCall mode, byte x, byte y, byte area, ESteamPacket type, int size, byte[] packet)` -- area-based
- `send(ESteamCall mode, Vector3 point, float radius, ESteamPacket type, int size, byte[] packet)` -- sphere-based

**Receive method**: `receive(CSteamID steamID, byte[] packet, int offset, int size)` -- validates against `ESteamCallValidation` attributes.

**RPC discovery**: `buildCallArray()` uses reflection to find methods decorated with `[SteamCall]` attributes, supporting `ClientInvocationContext` and `ServerInvocationContext` parameters.

Source: [Unturned-Datamining SteamChannel.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/SteamChannel.cs)

---

## 11. Current RPC System (Post-Rewrite)

From `PlayerLife.cs` and Issue #2479:

**Method handle declarations** use generic typed classes:
- `ClientInstanceMethod&lt;T1, T2, ...&gt;` -- server invokes on client
- `ServerInstanceMethod&lt;T1, ...&gt;` -- client invokes on server

Example declarations:
```
SendDeath: ClientInstanceMethod&lt;EDeathCause, ELimb, CSteamID&gt; -&gt; "ReceiveDeath"
SendRevive: ClientInstanceMethod&lt;Vector3, byte&gt; -&gt; "ReceiveRevive"
SendSuicideRequest: ServerInstanceMethod -&gt; "ReceiveSuicideRequest"
```

**Invocation**: `.Invoke(GetNetId(), ENetReliability.Reliable, connection, payload_args...)`

**Attributes**: `[SteamCall(ESteamCallValidation.ONLY_FROM_SERVER, legacyName = "tellDead")]` marks handler methods.

**NetId**: Replaces `SteamChannel.id` as unique identifier. Potentially 22-26 bits with recycling and indicator bits.

**Auto-generated code**: `_NetMethods` classes auto-generated in `Assets/Runtime/Assembly-CSharp/NetGen/` with read/write implementations for each RPC. These are excluded from Git.

Sources: [Unturned-Datamining PlayerLife.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/PlayerLife.cs), [RPC Rewrite Issue #2479](https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/2479)

---

## 12. NetPak Serialization System (Current)

Files in `SDG-NetPak-Runtime/SDG.NetPak/`:

**Core classes**: `NetPakWriter` and `NetPakReader`

**NetPakWriter internals**:
- `byte[] buffer` -- target data storage
- `ulong scratch` -- 64-bit accumulator for bit packing
- `int writeByteIndex` -- current write position
- `int scratchBitCount` -- bits in scratch
- Core methods: `WriteBit(bool)`, `WriteBits(uint, int bitCount)`, `WriteBytes(byte[], int, int)`, `Flush()`, `AlignToByte()`, `Reset()`

**Primitive write extensions** (`SystemNetPakWriterEx.cs`):
```
WriteInt8(sbyte)
WriteInt16(short)
WriteInt32(int)
WriteInt64(long)
WriteUInt8(byte)
WriteUInt16(ushort)
WriteUInt32(uint)
WriteUInt64(ulong)
WriteFloat(float)
WriteString(string, int lengthBitCount = 11)
WriteGuid(Guid)
WriteDateTime(DateTime)
```
Plus specialized: `WriteSignedInt`, `WriteUnsignedClampedFloat`, `WriteClampedFloat`, `WriteUnsignedNormalizedFloat`, `WriteSignedNormalizedFloat`, `WriteRadians`, `WriteDegrees`

**Unity write extensions** (`UnityNetPakWriterEx.cs`):
```
WriteQuaternion(Quaternion, bitsPerComponent=9) -- "smallest three" compression
WriteNormalVector3(Vector3, bitsPerComponent=9) -- largest component + 2 smaller
WriteClampedVector3(Vector3, intBitCount=13, fracBitCount=7) -- per-axis clamped float
WriteColor32RGB(Color32) -- 3x 8-bit channels
WriteColor32RGBA(Color32) -- 4x 8-bit channels
WriteSpecialYawOrQuaternion(Quaternion) -- yaw optimization for Y-axis only rotations
WriteNormalVector3AsYaw(Vector3, bitCount=16) -- direction as angle
WriteVector3AsYawMagnitude(Vector3, yawBitCount=16) -- direction + magnitude
```

**Steamworks write extensions** (`SteamworksNetPakWriterEx.cs`):
```
WriteSteamID(CSteamID) -- writes m_SteamID as uint64
WriteSteamID(PublishedFileId_t) -- writes m_PublishedFileId as uint64
WriteSteamItemDefID(SteamItemDef_t) -- writes as int32
WriteSteamItemInstanceID(SteamItemInstanceID_t) -- writes as uint64
```

**Enum serialization**: Enums marked with `[NetEnum]` attribute get auto-generated `WriteEnum`/`ReadEnum` extension methods. The `NetEnumAttribute` is a marker attribute that triggers code generation at build time.

**String encoding**: UTF-8, max 2048 bytes, length prefix uses 11 bits by default (`MAX_STRING_BYTE_COUNT_BITS = 11`, `MAX_STRING_BYTE_COUNT = 2048`).

**NetPakReader** mirrors all write methods with `Read` equivalents returning `bool` success with `out` parameters.

Sources: [NetPakConst.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/SDG-NetPak-Runtime/SDG.NetPak/NetPakConst.cs), [NetPakWriter.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/SDG-NetPak-Runtime/SDG.NetPak/NetPakWriter.cs), [SystemNetPakWriterEx.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/SDG-NetPak-Runtime/SDG.NetPak/SystemNetPakWriterEx.cs), [UnityNetPakWriterEx.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/SDG-NetPak-Runtime/SDG.NetPak/UnityNetPakWriterEx.cs)

---

## 13. Block Class (Legacy Serialization)

From `Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/Block.cs`:

**Buffer**: `BUFFER_SIZE = 65535` bytes (static)

**Supported types** with dedicated read/write methods:
- Primitives: `byte`, `Int16`, `Int32`, `Int64`, `UInt64`, `Single` (float), `Boolean`
- Collections: `byte[]`, `string[]`, `bool[]`, `int[]`, `UInt64[]`
- Complex: `CSteamID`, `Guid`, `Vector3`, `Quaternion`, `Color`
- Specialized: `UInt16RVector3` (region-based coordinate encoding)

**String serialization**: UTF-8 with length prefix.

**Byte array length prefix**: Single byte normally, Int32 if `longBinaryData` is true.

**Boolean arrays**: Bit-packed (8 booleans per byte).

**Hash**: SHA1 digest via `getHash()`.

Source: [Unturned-Datamining Block.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/Block.cs)

---

## 14. SteamPacker (Legacy)

From `Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/SteamPacker.cs`:

Now a deprecated wrapper over `NetPakBlockImplementation` (`luggageBlock`). Key methods:
- `getObjects(ClientInvocationContext/ServerInvocationContext, ...)` -- deserializes packet payload given type descriptors
- `openRead()`, `closeRead()`, `openWrite()`, `closeWrite()` -- buffer lifecycle
- Uses `Types.BYTE_TYPE`, `Types.STRING_TYPE`, `Types.BYTE_ARRAY_TYPE`, `Types.UINT32_TYPE`, `Types.BOOLEAN_TYPE`, `Types.SINGLE_TYPE`, `Types.STEAM_ID_TYPE`, `Types.COLOR_TYPE`, `Types.UINT64_TYPE`, `Types.UINT64_ARRAY_TYPE` constants

All public methods marked `[Obsolete]`.

Source: [Unturned-Datamining SteamPacker.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/SteamPacker.cs)

## 15. Provider -- Connection and Authentication

From `Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/Provider.cs`:

- Uses `IClientTransport` / `IServerTransport` / `ITransportConnection` abstractions
- `TransportConnectionRateLimiter` rate-limits problematic packets per connection
- Authentication flow uses `EServerMessage.Authenticate` and `EClientMessage.Accepted`
- Static `buffer` array sized at `Block.BUFFER_SIZE` (65535 bytes)
- `MAX_SKINS_LENGTH` = `NetLength(127)` bytes
- Two connection maps: `_transportConnectionToPlayerMap` (active) and `_transportConnectionToPendingPlayerMap` (queued)

Source: [Unturned-Datamining Provider.cs](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/blob/linux-client-preview/Assembly-CSharp/SDG.Unturned/Provider.cs)

## 16. Transport Layer

Files in `SDG-NetTransport/SDG.NetTransport/`:
- `IClientTransport.cs`, `IServerTransport.cs`, `ITransportConnection.cs` -- abstractions
- `TransportBase.cs` -- base class
- `ClientTransport_Null.cs`, `ClientTransportFailure.cs`, `ClientTransportReady.cs` -- state implementations
- Connection identification changed from `CSteamID` to opaque `ITransportConnection` (SteamIDs retained for queue/online player lookup)
- `ITransportConnection.TryGetIPv4Address()` and `TryGetPort()` replace `SteamGameServerNetworking` calls

Source: [Unturned-Datamining SDG-NetTransport](https://github.com/Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining/tree/linux-client-preview/SDG-NetTransport/SDG.NetTransport)

## 17. Deprecated Hooks

From Issue #1677: `onServerReadingPacket`, `onServerWritingPacket`, `onTriggerReceive`, and `onTriggerSend` are deprecated. A command-line flag was added to pass copied buffers to these delegates so plugins cannot write to the game's actual network traffic. They remain supported but are not ideal.

Source: [Deprecation Issue #1677](https://github.com/SmartlyDressedGames/Unturned-3.x-Community/issues/1677)

From community plugin examples:

```csharp
player.channel.send("askMessage", ESteamCall.OWNER, 
    ESteamPacket.UPDATE_UNRELIABLE_BUFFER, 
    new object[] { (byte)89 });

// Effect send pattern
EffectManager.instance.channel.send("tellEffectPoint", steamID, 
    ESteamPacket.UPDATE_UNRELIABLE_BUFFER, effectID, position);
```

The primary source for decompiled Unturned code is **Unturned-Datamining/Unturned-Datamining** on GitHub, which has permission to decompile and share the game code. It updates on every preview build. The repository structure includes:
